Use this whenever adding a new endpoint that takes a path/query parameter identifying a resource (student_id, drive_id, application_id, assessment_id, etc.), when reviewing an existing router file for authorization gaps, or when the user asks to check for IDOR, BOLA, "can user X see user Y's data," or resource ownership bugs. ([Sanin1024/AI-Engineering-Skills](https://github.com/Sanin1024/AI-Engineering-Skills/tree/HEAD/04-Security/idor-authorization-audit))
- **idor-automation-patterns**: Automate authorized IDOR/BOLA testing: two-account capture, ID extraction, parameter swapping, response diffing, rate-limited wordlists, and CI regression harnesses. Use after manual confirmation patterns exist on owned/lab APIs — complements idor-broken-object-authorization; not unsolicited mass scanning. ([bedkillerspacex-boop/codex-skill-library](https://github.com/bedkillerspacex-boop/codex-skill-library/tree/HEAD/idor-automation-patterns) · ★1)
- **idor-blast-radius**: When you find an Insecure Direct Object Reference (a URL/body/header parameter that lets you read or write another user's or another tenant's object), discover the ID space, prove the access is unauthorized, quantify the blast radius (how many records reachable, what data class, read vs write, same-tenant vs cross-tenant), and persist a finding sized by real impact rather than by the existence of the flaw.
